{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-6","id":"msg_01VmiDmpA9SviQrUxh2RPac5","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"thinking","thinking":"Now I understand. The `preprocess_observation_pytorch` function handles the image format. It checks if `image.shape[1] == 3` (channels first) and if so, permutes to HWC for processing, then permutes back at the end.\n\nIf the image arrives as `[B, H, W, C]` (i.e., `image.shape[1]` is 224, not 3), then `is_channels_first = False`, and at the end it stays in HWC format. Then `embed_image` passes it to the SigLIP vision tower which expects BCHW.\n\n\nSo the bug is clear: when images come in as HWC from `obs_to_model_input`, the function never converts them to CHW before sending to the model. The issue is that the permutation back to channels-first only happens if `is_channels_first` was true initially, but for HWC inputs that flag is false, so the image stays in the wrong format.\n\nThe vision tower expects CHW, but during inference with `policy.infer()`, the numpy arrays from `obs_to_model_input` are HWC uint8, and somewhere in the conversion pipeline this format mismatch isn't being corrected. {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-6","id":"msg_01VYaS8CWxbkmccu24D4btcG","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"thinking","thinking":"I see it now! Line 78:\n```python\ninputs = jax.tree.map(lambda x: torch.from_numpy(np.array(x)).to(self._pytorch_device)[None, ...], inputs)\n```\n\nThis converts numpy arrays to tensors and adds a batch dimension. If the image is `(224, 224, 3)` numpy array, it becomes `(1, 224, 224, 3)` tensor — HWC with batch dim. Then in `_preprocess_observation`:\n```python\nis_channels_first = image.shape[1] == 3  # Check if channels are in dimension 1\n\n\n```\n\nSince `shape[1] = 224` (not 3), `is_channels_first = False`, and the image stays HWC. Then it goes to `embed_image` which passes it to SigLIP's `get_image_features` which expects BCHW — that's the bug right there. The original `collect_activations.py` worked with pi0_base, so either the original loading path handled this differently or `obs_to_model_input` returns images in a different format already. Let me trace what `obs_to_model_input` actually returns.\n\nSo `_resize_with_pad` outputs `(224, 224, 3)` uint8 numpy arrays, and that's what gets passed through. The image then flows through LiberoInputs which restructures it, then ResizeImages which is a no-op since it's already 224x224, before hitting the policy.\n\n```\n\npython\ndef _resize_with_pad(img: np.ndarray, size: int) -> np.ndarray:\n    \"\"\"Resize image with aspect-preserving padding to (size, size, 3) uint8.\"\"\"\n```\n\nDuring inference, the `.infer` call adds a batch dimension making it `(1, 224, 224, 3)`, then `preprocess_observation_pytorch` doesn't permute because `is_channels_first = False`, so it stays in HWC format. But SigLIP expects CHW format `(1, 3, 224, 224)`, which means there's a shape mismatch causing a crash.\n\nI need to check if `_parse_image` in LiberoInputs is doing the channel conversion to openpi.models_pytorch import preprocessing_pytorch as pp\n    _orig = pp.preprocess_observation_pytorch\n\n    def patched(observation, *, train=False, image_keys=pp.IMAGE_KEYS, image_resolution=pp.IMAGE_RESOLUTION):\n        result = _orig(observation, train=train, image_keys=image_keys, image_resolution=image_resolution)\n        # Force CHW format for all images\n        new_images = {}\n        for key, image in result.images.items():\n            if image.ndim == 4 and image.shape[-1] == 3:  # BHWC\n                image = image.permute(0, 3, 1, 2).contiguous()\n            new_images[key] = image\n        result.images = new_images\n        return result\n\n    pp.preprocess_observation_pytorch = patched\n\n\ndef load_pi0_libero(checkpoint_path: str, device: str):\n    \"\"\"Load pi0_libero with model.* prefix stripping, no torch.compile.\"\"\"\n    _ensure_openpi_imports()\n\n    from openpi.training import config as _config\n    from _config.get_config(\"pi0_libero\")\n    model_config = dataclasses.replace(train_config.model, pytorch_compile_mode=None)\n\n    model = pi0_pytorch.PI0Pytorch(config=model_config)\n\n    weight_path = os.path.join(checkpoint_path, \"model.safetensors\")\n    tensors = safetensors.torch.load_file(weight_path)\n\n    skip_prefixes = ('normalize_inputs.', 'normalize_targets.', 'unnormalize_outputs.')\n    cleaned = {}\n    for k, v in tensors.items():\n        if any(k.startswith(p) for p in skip_prefixes):\n            continue\n        cleaned[k.removeprefix('model.')] = model.paligemma_with_expert.to_bfloat16_for_selected_params(\"bfloat16\")\n    model.to(device)\n    model.eval()\n\n    data_config = train_config.data.create(train_config.assets_dirs, train_config.model)\n\n    policy = _policy.Policy(\n        model,\n        transforms=[\n            *data_config.data_transforms.inputs,\n            transforms.Normalize(None, use_quantiles=False),\n            *data_config.model_transforms.inputs,\n        ],\n        output_transforms=[\n            *data_config.model_transforms.outputs,\n            transforms.Unnormalize(None, use_quantiles=False),\n            *data_config.data_transforms.outputs,\n        ],\n        is_pytorch=True,\n        pytorch_device=device,\n    )\n\n    log.info(\"Loaded pi0_libero on %s (missing=%d keys)\", device, len(missing))\n    return policy, model\n\n\nfrom libero_integration import (\n    create_libero_env,\n    obs_to_model_input,\n    LIBERO_DUMMY_ACTION,\n    MAX_STEPS,\n    DEFAULT_REPLAN_STEPS,\n    DEFAULT_NUM_STEPS_WAIT,\n)\nfrom collect_activations import ActivationCollector\n\n\ndef run_episode_per_step(\n    policy, collector, perturbation_type, device,\n    task_suite=\"libero_spatial\", seed=42,\n    replan_steps=DEFAULT_REPLAN_STEPS,\n    num_steps_wait=DEFAULT_NUM_STEPS_WAIT,\n):\n    env, task_description, init_states, task_idx = create_libero_env(\n        task_suite_name=task_suite,\n        perturbation_type=perturbation_type,\n        seed=seed,\n    )\n    max_steps = MAX_STEPS.get(task_suite, 400)\n    step = 0\n    success = False\n\n    try:\n        trial_idx = random.randint(0, len(init_states) - 1)\n        obs = env.set_init_state(init_states[trial_idx])\n\n        for _ in range(num_steps_wait):\n            obs, _, _, _ = env.step(LIBERO_DUMMY_ACTION)\n\n        action_plan = collections.deque()\n        step_activations = []\n        done = False\n\n        while not done and step < max_steps:\n            if len(action_plan) == 0:\n                collector.clear()\n                obs_dict = obs_to_model_input(obs, task_description)\n                result = policy.infer(obs_dict)\n                action_chunk = result[\"actions\"]\n                action_plan.extend(action_chunk[:replan_steps])\n\n                pooled = collector.get_pooled()\n                if pooled:\n                    step_activations.append(pooled)\n\n            action = action_plan.popleft()\n            if isinstance(action, torch.Tensor):\n                action = action.cpu().numpy()\n            obs, reward, done, info = env.step(\n                action.tolist() if hasattr(action, \"tolist\") else list(action)\n            )\n            step += 1\n            if done or reward > 0:\n                success = info.get(\"success\", reward > 0)\n    finally:\n        env.close()\n\n    return {\n        \"step_activations\": step_activations,\n        \"num_replan_points\": len(step_activations),\n        \"task_name\": f\"{task_suite}/task_{task_idx}\",\n        \"task_description\": task_description,\n        \"num_steps\": step,\n        \"success\": success,\n    }\n\n\ndef make_labels(perturbation_type):\n    if perturbation_type == \"nominal\":\n        return {\"label_6class\": -1, \"label_3class\": -1, \"label_binary\": 0}\n    label_6 = PERTURBATION_TYPES_6CLASS[perturbation_type]\n    label_3 = CLASS_6_TO_3_MAPPING[label_6]\n    return {\"label_6class\": label_6, \"label_3class\": label_3, \"label_binary\": 1}\n\n\ndef collect(args):\n    random.seed(args.seed)\n    np.random.seed(args.seed)\n    torch.manual_seed(args.seed)\n\n    output_dir = Path(args.output_dir)\n    output_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n\n    log.info(\"Loading pi0_libero from %s on %s\", args.checkpoint, args.device)\n    policy, model = load_pi0_libero(args.checkpoint, args.device)\n\n    collector = ActivationCollector(model)\n    collector.register_hooks()\n\n    if args.perturbation_type == \"all\":\n        perturbation_types = list(PERTURBATION_TYPES_6CLASS.keys())\n    else:\n        perturbation_types = [args.perturbation_type]\n\n    for ptype in perturbation_types:\n        is_nominal = ptype == \"nominal\"\n        log.info(\"Collecting %d episodes for %s\", args.num_episodes, ptype)\n        episodes = []\n\n        for ep_idx in range(args.num_episodes):\n            log.info(\"  Episode %d/%d\", ep_idx + 1, args.num_episodes)\n            try:\n                result = run_episode_per_step(\n                    policy, collector,\n                    perturbation_type=None if is_nominal else ptype,\n                    device=args.device,\n                    task_suite=args.task_suite,\n                    seed=args.seed + ep_idx,\n                )\n            except Exception:\n                log.exception(\"Episode %d failed, skipping\", ep_idx)\n                continue\n\n            labels = make_labels(ptype)\n            episode_data = {\n                \"step_activations\": result[\"step_activations\"],\n                \"num_replan_points\": result[\"num_replan_points\"],\n                **labels,\n                \"metadata\": {\n                    \"perturbation_type\": ptype,\n                    \"task_name\": result[\"task_name\"],\n                    \"num_steps\": result[\"num_steps\"],\n                    \"success\": result[\"success\"],\n                    \"episode_idx\": ep_idx,\n                },\n            }\n            episodes.append(episode_data)\n\n        save_path = output_dir / f\"{ptype}.pt\"\n        torch.save(episodes, save_path)\n        total_steps = sum(ep[\"num_replan_points\"] for ep in episodes)\n        log.info(\"Saved %s: %d episodes, %d total steps\", save_path, len(episodes), total_steps)\n\n    collector.remove_hooks()\n    log.info(\"Collection complete.\")\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    logging.basicConfig(\n        level=logging.INFO,\n        format=\"%(asctime)s [%(levelname)s] %(name)s: %(message)s\",\n    )\n    p = argparse.ArgumentParser()\n    p.add_argument(\"--checkpoint\", type=str,\n                   default=\"/root/autodl-tmp/checkpoints/pi0_libero\")\n    p.add_argument(\"--output_dir\", type=str,\n                   default=\"/root/meta_brain_lora_routing/activations_v2/per_step\")\n    valid_types = list(PERTURBATION_TYPES_6CLASS.keys()) + [\"all\", \"nominal\"]\n    p.add_argument(\"--perturbation_type\", type=str, default=\"all\", choices=valid_types)\n    p.add_argument(\"--num_episodes\", type=int, default=25)\n    p.add_argument(\"--task_suite\", type=str, default=\"libero_spatial\",\n                   choices=[\"libero_spatial\", \"libero_object\", \"libero_goal\", \"libero_10\", \"libero_90\"])\n    p.add_argument(\"--device\", type=str, default=\"cuda:1\")\n    p.add_argument(\"--seed\", type=int, default=42)\n    args = p.parse_args()\n    collect(args)\nSCRIPT","account":"root"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":1,"cache_creation_input_tokens":374,"cache_read_input_tokens":127088,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":374},"output_tokens":8,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"436bc48d-6197-44c5-bb0b-d9d9fb2582a8","uuid":"baa8f561-bd8e-4fa2-99aa-7ff010b7795f"}

{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-6","id":"msg_013tmbWuq2nDmuESmXxqZxn8","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_011uosLn6gj3fWk6HRdCK4wu","name":"mcp__agent-ml-research__ssh_execute","input":{"server":"westd-34142","command":"cat > /root/meta_brain_lora_routing/phase1/train_par_router_v2.sh << 'EOF'\n#!/bin/bash\n# Train PAR Router v2 on pi0_libero activations + evaluate old router on new data\nsource /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh && conda activate base\n\ncd /root/meta_brain_lora_routing\n\necho \"=== Step 3: Train new Router on v2 activations ===\"\npython3 phase1/train_par_router.py \\\n    --data_dir /root/meta_brain_lora_routing/activations_v2/per_step \\\n    --layer paligemma_layer_6 \\\n    --pooling first \\\n    --gpu 1 \\\n    --epochs 50 \\\n    --lr 1e-3 \\\n    --checkpoint_dir /root/meta_brain_lora_routing/checkpoints/phase1/par_router_v2 \\\n    --output_dir /root/meta_brain_lora_routing/phase1/outputs_v2\n\necho \"\"\necho \"=== Step 4: Evaluate old Router (v1) on new (v2) activations ===\"\npython3 -c \"\nimport torch\nimport numpy as np\nfrom pathlib import Path\nfrom sklearn.metrics import f1_score, classification_report\nfrom collections import Counter\nimport sys\nsys.path.insert(0, 'phase1')\nfrom train_par_router import PARRouter, load_per_step_data\n\n# Load v2 data\nX, y = load_per_step_data(\n    Path('/root/meta_brain_lora_routing/activations_v2/per_step'),\n    layer='paligemma_layer_6', pooling='first'\n)\nprint(f'v2 Dataset: X={X.shape}, y={y.shape}')\nprint(f'Class distribution: {dict(Counter(y.tolist()))}')\n\n# Load old router (v1)\nckpt = torch.load('/root/meta_brain_lora_routing/checkpoints/phase1/par_router/best_router.pt',\n                  map_location='cpu', weights_only=False)\nmodel = PARRouter(\n    input_dim=ckpt['config']['input_dim'],\n    hidden_dim=ckpt['config']['hidden_dim'],\n    num_classes=ckpt['config']['num_classes'],\n    dropout=ckpt['config']['dropout'],\n)\nmodel.load_state_dict(ckpt['model_state_dict'])\nmodel.eval()\n\n# Predict\nwith torch.no_grad():\n    logits = model(X)\n    preds = logits.argmax(dim=1).numpy()\n\ny_np = y.numpy()\nf1 = f1_score(y_np, preds, average='macro')\nprint(f'\\\\nOld Router (v1) on new activations (v2): macro-F1 = {f1:.4f}')\nprint(f'Old Router (v1) on old activations (v1): macro-F1 = {ckpt[\\\"metrics\\\"][\\\"mean_f1\\\"]:.4f}')\nprint(f'Degradation: {ckpt[\\\"metrics\\\"][\\\"mean_f1\\\"] - f1:.4f}')\nprint()\nprint(classification_report(y_np, preds, target_names=['visual', 'spatial', 'physical'], digits=4))\n\"\nEOF\nchmod +x /root/meta_brain_lora_routing/phase1/train_par_router_v2.sh","account":"root"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":1,"cache_creation_input_tokens":240,"cache_read_input_tokens":141511,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":240},"output_tokens":1,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"436bc48d-6197-44c5-bb0b-d9d9fb2582a8","uuid":"e88c059d-e57c-421d-97ba-a47c2bfd8198"}

{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"type":"text","text":"This session is being continued from a previous conversation that ran out of context. The summary below covers the earlier portion of the conversation.\n\nSummary:\n1. Primary Request and Intent:\n   The user requested Phase B.5: collect PaliGemma layer 6 activations from pi0_libero (fine-tuned model) and retrain the PAR Router. The original Router was trained on pi0_base activations (5-fold F1=0.9963). Since Phase C confirmed pi0_libero modified all 777 PaliGemma weights, the Router must be retrained on the new activation distribution. Steps: (1) find existing code, (2) collect pi0_libero activations into `activations_v2/per_step/`, (3) train new Router v2, (4) compare old vs new Router performance.\n\n2. Key Technical Concepts:\n   - PI0 model architecture: PaliGemma (vision-language) + Gemma Expert (action)\n   - LeRobot vs OpenPI checkpoint formats (model.* prefix in keys)\n   - Forward hooks for activation extraction on transformer layers\n   - Per-step (per-replan-point) activation collection during LIBERO rollouts\n   - PaliGemma layer 6 with first-token pooling → 2048-dim features\n   - PAR Router: 2-layer MLP (2048→256→3) for 3-class classification (visual/spatial/physical)\n   - 5-fold cross-validation with macro-F1 metric\n   - torch.compile incompatibility requiring disable via `pytorch_compile_mode=None`\n   - Image format issue: HWC vs CHW in preprocessing_pytorch.py\n\n3. Files and Code Sections:\n   - `/root/meta_brain_lora_routing/phase0/collect_activations_v2.py`\n     - The main working script for collecting per-step activations from pi0_libero\n     - Handles LeRobot-format checkpoint loading with `model.` prefix stripping\n     - Disables torch.compile, patches preprocessing for HWC→CHW conversion\n     - Key function `load_pi0_libero()` creates model, strips keys, builds Policy\n     - Key function `_patch_preprocessing()` monkey-patches preprocessing to force CHW output\n     - Full working code deployed on server\n\n   - `/root/meta_brain_lora_routing/phase1/train_par_router.py`\n     - Existing router training script\n     - Loads per-step data via `load_per_step_data(data_dir, layer, pooling)`\n     - PARRouter class: Linear(2048→256) + ReLU + Dropout(0.1) + Linear(256→3)\n     - 5-fold StratifiedKFold cross-validation\n     - Args: --data_dir, --layer, --pooling, --gpu, --checkpoint_dir, --output_dir\n\n   - `/root/meta_brain_lora_routing/phase1/train_par_router_v2.sh`\n     - Prepared bash script for Step 3 (train v2 router) and Step 4 (evaluate old router on new data)\n     - Trains with: --data_dir activations_v2/per_step --layer paligemma_layer_6 --pooling first --gpu 1\n     - Evaluates old router (v1) on v2 activations and reports degradation\n\n   - `/root/meta_brain_lora_routing/phase0/libero_integration.py`\n     - `load_pi0_for_collection(checkpoint_path, device, config_name=\"pi0_libero\")` - original loader\n     - `run_episode()` - runs LIBERO rollout, returns averaged activations\n     - `create_libero_env()` - creates perturbation-specific LIBERO environments\n     - `obs_to_model_input()` - converts LIBERO observations to policy input format\n\n   - `/root/meta_brain_lora_routing/phase0/configs.py`\n     - PERTURBATION_TYPES_6CLASS: camera_viewpoints=0, light_conditions=1, background_textures=2, sensor_noise=3, objects_layout=4, robot_initial_states=5\n     - CLASS_6_TO_3_MAPPING: {0-3: visual(0), 4: spatial(1), 5: physical(2)}\n     - PALIGEMMA_LAYERS = [2, 4, 6, 8, 10, 12, 14, 16]\n     - PALIGEMMA_LAYER_TEMPLATE = \"paligemma_with_expert.paligemma.language_model.layers.{i}\"\n\n   - `/root/openpi/src/openpi/models_pytorch/preprocessing_pytorch.py`\n     - Has a bug: HWC inputs stay HWC when `is_channels_first=False`, never converts to CHW for model\n     - Fixed via monkey-patch in collect_activations_v2.py\n\n   - `/root/autodl-tmp/checkpoints/pi0_libero_openpi/model.safetensors`\n     - Converted checkpoint with stripped `model.` prefix (777 keys)\n     - Not actually used since collect_activations_v2.py does stripping in-memory\n\n4. Errors and fixes:\n   - **Keys mismatch (model.* prefix)**: pi0_libero checkpoint saved in LeRobot format with `model.` prefix + normalize buffers. Fixed by stripping `model.` prefix and skipping normalize_inputs/normalize_targets/unnormalize_outputs keys during load_state_dict.\n   - **Wrong module path**: `openpi.models.pi0` doesn't have `PI0Pytorch`. Fixed: correct import is `from openpi.models_pytorch import pi0_pytorch` → class `pi0_pytorch.PI0Pytorch`.\n   - **Wrong transforms import**: `from openpi.shared import transforms` doesn't exist. Fixed: `import openpi.transforms as transforms`.\n   - **torch.compile failure**: `pytorch_compile_mode='max-autotune'` in pi0_libero config causes dynamo errors during inference. Fixed: `dataclasses.replace(train_config.model, pytorch_compile_mode=None)` + `torch._dynamo.config.suppress_errors = True`.\n   - **Image HWC→CHW**: Images arrive as (B,224,224,3) but SigLIP expects (B,3,224,224). `preprocessing_pytorch.py` detects `is_channels_first=False` but never converts back to CHW. Fixed: monkey-patch `preprocess_observation_pytorch` to permute all HWC images to CHW after processing.\n   - **Missing key**: `paligemma_with_expert.paligemma.model.language_model.embed_tokens.weight` is missing (1 key) — this is a tied/shared weight, not critical.\n   - **Norm stats not found**: Warned but gracefully skipped. Not critical for activation collection since PaliGemma layer 6 activations are primarily driven by visual tokens.\n\n5. Problem Solving:\n   - Successfully solved the pi0_libero checkpoint loading problem (5 iterations)\n   - Smoke test passed: 2 episodes → 88 steps with correct activation shapes\n   - Full collection running: camera_viewpoints (25 eps, 1100 steps) completed\n   - Router training script prepared and ready to run after collection completes\n\n6. All user messages:\n   - First message: Detailed task description for Phase B.5 with 4 steps, specifying server (westd-34142), paths, parameters (cuda:1, layer 6, first-token pooling, 25 episodes per type), output directories, and expected deliverables.\n\n7. Pending Tasks:\n   - Wait for activation collection to complete (5 more perturbation types × 25 episodes each, ~25 min remaining)\n   - Run router training (Step 3): train_par_router.py on v2 activations\n   - Run comparison (Step 4): evaluate old router on new activations, report degradation\n   - Report final deliverables: activation stats, new Router F1, old Router degradation, checkpoint path\n\n8. Total remaining ~25 min.\n   \n   After collection completes, need to run:\n   ```bash\n   bash /root/meta_brain_lora_routing/phase1/train_par_router_v2.sh\n   ```\n   This trains the new router and evaluates old router on new data.\n\n9.
